rating: 4
review_text: We have lived in the area a long time and decided to try the Galley out since we heard to many great reviews.  We got there for a late lunch since we thought it would be slow after the lunch rush. We liked the initial greeting but it start to get a little busy and took a while for them to take our order. We notice the customer was waiting for his food that was in the good windows but I  guess there was a loss of communication between the staff. So the food sat there 10 minutes before he was able to ask if he could get his food. We finally ordered food, chicken wings and a pizza. The wings could have been pre seasoned instead of just having a smoky taste with a taste of sauce. The pizza was pretty good. We saw they had live music from 2 to 5 on Saturday and Sunday. We might have to check this out. We think it's  a nice little place to hang out. We plan on returning here and see  the live band, hopefully they can improve on service. On a side note, we did notice a staff member get a cup of draft beer and take it to the back. Those optics are hard to ignore.

entity_slug: the-galley-on-the-river
reviewer_name: Daniel Villalobos
rating: 4
body: We have lived in the area a long time and decided to try the Galley out since we heard to many great reviews.  We got there for a late lunch since we thought it would be slow after the lunch rush. We liked the initial greeting but it start to get a little busy and took a while for them to take our order. We notice the customer was waiting for his food that was in the good windows but I  guess there was a loss of communication between the staff. So the food sat there 10 minutes before he was able to ask if he could get his food. We finally ordered food, chicken wings and a pizza. The wings could have been pre seasoned instead of just having a smoky taste with a taste of sauce. The pizza was pretty good. We saw they had live music from 2 to 5 on Saturday and Sunday. We might have to check this out. We think it's  a nice little place to hang out. We plan on returning here and see  the live band, hopefully they can improve on service. On a side note, we did notice a staff member get a cup of draft beer and take it to the back. Those optics are hard to ignore.
